CMD:kart(playerid,params[])
	{
        new Name[25], string[128];
		GetPlayerName(playerid,Name,sizeof(Name));
		format(string, sizeof(string),"{00FF00}Hrбč {FF0000}%s{00FF00} se portnul na {FF0000} VG - S.W.A.T {00FF00}[ /SWAT ]{FF0000} by Simon...", Name);
		SendClientMessageToAll(0xFF0000FF, string);
		SetPlayerPos(playerid, -2061.8245,-107.5849,35.3203);
		return 1;
    }

		SendClientMessageToAll(0xFF0000FF, string);
		SetPlayerPos(playerid, 1428.67,-851.05,49.76);
		return 1;
}
